  const n = Math.floor(e), i = e - n, s = l * (1 - t), o = l * (1 - i * t), d = l * (1 - (1 - i) * t), r = n % 6, u = [l, o, s, s, d, l][r], c = [d, l, l, o, s, s][r], y = [s, s, d, l, l, o][r];
  return { r: u * 255, g: c * 255, b: y * 255 };
}
function ia(e, t, l, n) {
  const i = hl(Math.round(e).toString(16)), s = hl(Math.round(t).toString(16)), o = hl(Math.round(l).toString(16));
  return n && i.startsWith(i.charAt(1)) && s.startsWith(s.charAt(1)) && o.startsWith(o.charAt(1)) ? i.charAt(0) + s.charAt(0) + o.charAt(0) : i + s + o;
}
function Wc(e, t, l, n, i) {
  const s = hl(Math.round(e).toString(16)), o = hl(Math.round(t).toString(16)), d = hl(Math.round(l).toString(16)), r = hl(Hc(n));
  return i && s.startsWith(s.charAt(1)) && o.startsWith(o.charAt(1)) && d.startsWith(d.charAt(1)) && r.startsWith(r.charAt(1)) ? s.charAt(0) + o.charAt(0) + d.charAt(0) + r.charAt(0) : s + o + d + r;
}
function Bc(e, t, l, n) {
  const i = e / 100, s = t / 100, o = l / 100, d = n / 100, r = 255 * (1 - i) * (1 - d), u = 255 * (1 - s) * (1 - d), c = 255 * (1 - o) * (1 - d);
  return { r, g: u, b: c };
}
function sa(e, t, l) {
  let n = 1 - e / 255, i = 1 - t / 255, s = 1 - l / 255, o = Math.min(n, i, s);
  return o === 1 ? (n = 0, i = 0, s = 0) : (n = (n - o) / (1 - o) * 100, i = (i - o) / (1 - o) * 100, s = (s - o) / (1 - o) * 100), o *= 100, {
    c: Math.round(n),
    m: Math.round(i),
    y: Math.round(s),
    k: Math.round(o)
  };
}
function Hc(e) {
  return Math.round(parseFloat(e) * 255).toString(16);
}
function aa(e) {
  return ct(e) / 255;
}
function ct(e) {
  return parseInt(e, 16);
}
function Uc(e) {
  return {
    r: e >> 16,
    g: (e & 65280) >> 8,
    b: e & 255
  };
}

function qc(e) {
  let t = { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 }, l = 1, n = null, i = null, s = null, o = !1, d = !1;
  return typeof e == "string" && (e = Gc(e)), typeof e == "object" && (ft(e.r) && ft(e.g) && ft(e.b) ? (t = Fc(e.r, e.g, e.b), o = !0, d = String(e.r).substr(-1) === "%" ? "prgb" : "rgb") : ft(e.h) && ft(e.s) && ft(e.v) ? (n = pn(e.s), i = pn(e.v), t = Vc(e.h, n, i), o = !0, d = "hsv") : ft(e.h) && ft(e.s) && ft(e.l) ? (n = pn(e.s), s = pn(e.l), t = Nc(e.h, n, s), o = !0, d = "hsl") : ft(e.c) && ft(e.m) && ft(e.y) && ft(e.k) && (t = Bc(e.c, e.m, e.y, e.k), o = !0, d = "cmyk"), Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(e, "a") && (l = e.a)), l = No(l), {
    ok: o,
    format: e.format || d,
    r: Math.min(255, Math.max(t.r, 0)),
    g: Math.min(255, Math.max(t.g, 0)),
    b: Math.min(255, Math.max(t.b, 0)),
    a: l
  };
}
const zc = "[-\\+]?\\d+%?", Kc = "[-\\+]?\\d*\\.\\d+%?", tl = "(?:" + Kc + ")|(?:" + zc + ")", wi = "[\\s|\\(]+(" + tl + ")[,|\\s]+(" + tl + ")[,|\\s]+(" + tl + ")\\s*\\)?", wn = (
  // eslint-disable-next-line prettier/prettier
  "[\\s|\\(]+(" + tl + ")[,|\\s]+(" + tl + ")[,|\\s]+(" + tl + ")[,|\\s]+(" + tl + ")\\s*\\)?"
), mt = {
  hex: /^[0-9a-fA-F]+$/,
  CSS_UNIT: new RegExp(tl),
  rgb: new RegExp("rgb" + wi),
  rgba: new RegExp("rgba" + wn),
  hsl: new RegExp("hsl" + wi),
  hsla: new RegExp("hsla" + wn),
  hsv: new RegExp("hsv" + wi),
  hsva: new RegExp("hsva" + wn),
  cmyk: new RegExp("cmyk" + wn),
  hex3: /^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{1})([0-9a-fA-F]{1})([0-9a-fA-F]{1})$/,
  hex6: /^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{2})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})$/,
  hex4: /^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{1})([0-9a-fA-F]{1})([0-9a-fA-F]{1})([0-9a-fA-F]{1})$/,
  hex8: /^#?([0-9a-fA-F]{2})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})([0-9a-fA-F]{2})$/
};
function Gc(e) {
  if (e = e.trim().toLowerCase(), e.length === 0)
    return !1;
  let t = !1;
  if (Li[e])
    e = Li[e], t = !0;
  else if (e === "transparent")
    return { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, a: 0, format: "name" };
  let l;
  if (typeof e == "string" && e.length <= 9 && (e.startsWith("#") || mt.hex.test(e))) {
    if (l = mt.hex8.exec(e), l)
      return {
        r: ct(l[1]),
        g: ct(l[2]),
        b: ct(l[3]),
        a: aa(l[4]),
        format: t ? "name" : "hex8"
      };
    if (l = mt.hex6.exec(e), l)
      return {
        r: ct(l[1]),
        g: ct(l[2]),
        b: ct(l[3]),
        format: t ? "name" : "hex"
      };
    if (l = mt.hex4.exec(e), l)
      return {
        r: ct(l[1] + l[1]),
        g: ct(l[2] + l[2]),
        b: ct(l[3] + l[3]),
        a: aa(l[4] + l[4]),
        format: t ? "name" : "hex8"
      };
    if (l = mt.hex3.exec(e), l)
      return {
        r: ct(l[1] + l[1]),
        g: ct(l[2] + l[2]),
        b: ct(l[3] + l[3]),
        format: t ? "name" : "hex"
      };
  }
  return l = mt.rgb.exec(e), l ? { r: l[1], g: l[2], b: l[3] } : (l = mt.rgba.exec(e), l ? { r: l[1], g: l[2], b: l[3], a: l[4] } : (l = mt.hsl.exec(e), l ? { h: l[1], s: l[2], l: l[3] } : (l = mt.hsla.exec(e), l ? { h: l[1], s: l[2], l: l[3], a: l[4] } : (l = mt.hsv.exec(e), l ? { h: l[1], s: l[2], v: l[3] } : (l = mt.hsva.exec(e), l ? { h: l[1], s: l[2], v: l[3], a: l[4] } : (l = mt.cmyk.exec(e), l ? {
    c: l[1],
    m: l[2],
    y: l[3],
    k: l[4]
  } : !1))))));
}
function ft(e) {
  return typeof e == "number" ? !Number.isNaN(e) : mt.CSS_UNIT.test(e);
}
class Ve {
  constructor(t = "", l = {}) {
    if (t instanceof Ve)
      return t;
    typeof t == "number" && (t = Uc(t)), this.originalInput = t;
    const n = qc(t);
    this.originalInput = t, this.r = n.r, this.g = n.g, this.b = n.b, this.a = n.a, this.roundA = Math.round(100 * this.a) / 100, this.format = l.format ?? n.format, this.gradientType = l.gradientType, this.r < 1 && (this.r = Math.round(this.r)), this.g < 1 && (this.g = Math.round(this.g)), this.b < 1 && (this.b = Math.round(this.b)), this.isValid = n.ok;
  }
  isDark() {
    return this.getBrightness() < 128;
  }
  isLight() {
    return !this.isDark();
  }
  /**
   * Returns the perceived brightness of the color, from 0-255.
   */
  getBrightness() {
    const t = this.toRgb();
    return (t.r * 299 + t.g * 587 + t.b * 114) / 1e3;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the perceived luminance of a color, from 0-1.
   */
  getLuminance() {
    const t = this.toRgb();
    let l, n, i;
    const s = t.r / 255, o = t.g / 255, d = t.b / 255;
    return s <= 0.03928 ? l = s / 12.92 : l = Math.pow((s + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4), o <= 0.03928 ? n = o / 12.92 : n = Math.pow((o + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4), d <= 0.03928 ? i = d / 12.92 : i = Math.pow((d + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4), 0.2126 * l + 0.7152 * n + 0.0722 * i;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the alpha value of a color, from 0-1.
   */
  getAlpha() {
    return this.a;
  }
  /**
   * Sets the alpha value on the current color.
   *
   * @param alpha - The new alpha value. The accepted range is 0-1.
   */
  setAlpha(t) {
    return this.a = No(t), this.roundA = Math.round(100 * this.a) / 100, this;
  }
  /**
   * Returns whether the color is monochrome.
   */
  isMonochrome() {
    const { s: t } = this.toHsl();
    return t === 0;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a HSVA object.
   */
  toHsv() {
    const t = na(this.r, this.g, this.b);
    return { h: t.h * 360, s: t.s, v: t.v, a: this.a };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hsva values interpolated into a string with the following format:
   * "hsva(xxx, xxx, xxx, xx)".
   */
  toHsvString() {
    const t = na(this.r, this.g, this.b), l = Math.round(t.h * 360), n = Math.round(t.s * 100), i = Math.round(t.v * 100);
    return this.a === 1 ? `hsv(${l}, ${n}%, ${i}%)` : `hsva(${l}, ${n}%, ${i}%, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a HSLA object.
   */
  toHsl() {
    const t = la(this.r, this.g, this.b);
    return { h: t.h * 360, s: t.s, l: t.l, a: this.a };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hsla values interpolated into a string with the following format:
   * "hsla(xxx, xxx, xxx, xx)".
   */
  toHslString() {
    const t = la(this.r, this.g, this.b), l = Math.round(t.h * 360), n = Math.round(t.s * 100), i = Math.round(t.l * 100);
    return this.a === 1 ? `hsl(${l}, ${n}%, ${i}%)` : `hsla(${l}, ${n}%, ${i}%, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hex value of the color.
   * @param allow3Char will shorten hex value to 3 char if possible
   */
  toHex(t = !1) {
    return ia(this.r, this.g, this.b, t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hex value of the color -with a # prefixed.
   * @param allow3Char will shorten hex value to 3 char if possible
   */
  toHexString(t = !1) {
    return "#" + this.toHex(t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hex 8 value of the color.
   * @param allow4Char will shorten hex value to 4 char if possible
   */
  toHex8(t = !1) {
    return Wc(this.r, this.g, this.b, this.a, t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the hex 8 value of the color -with a # prefixed.
   * @param allow4Char will shorten hex value to 4 char if possible
   */
  toHex8String(t = !1) {
    return "#" + this.toHex8(t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the shorter hex value of the color depends on its alpha -with a # prefixed.
   * @param allowShortChar will shorten hex value to 3 or 4 char if possible
   */
  toHexShortString(t = !1) {
    return this.a === 1 ? this.toHexString(t) : this.toHex8String(t);
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a RGBA object.
   */
  toRgb() {
    return {
      r: Math.round(this.r),
      g: Math.round(this.g),
      b: Math.round(this.b),
      a: this.a
    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA values interpolated into a string with the following format:
   * "RGBA(xxx, xxx, xxx, xx)".
   */
  toRgbString() {
    const t = Math.round(this.r), l = Math.round(this.g), n = Math.round(this.b);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t}, ${l}, ${n})` : `rgba(${t}, ${l}, ${n}, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  /**
   * Returns the object as a RGBA object.
   */
  toPercentageRgb() {
    const t = (l) => `${Math.round(Je(l, 255) * 100)}%`;
    return {
      r: t(this.r),
      g: t(this.g),
      b: t(this.b),
      a: this.a
    };
  }
  /**
   * Returns the RGBA relative values interpolated into a string
   */
  toPercentageRgbString() {
    const t = (l) => Math.round(Je(l, 255) * 100);
    return this.a === 1 ? `rgb(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%)` : `rgba(${t(this.r)}%, ${t(this.g)}%, ${t(this.b)}%, ${this.roundA})`;
  }
  toCmyk() {
    return {
      ...sa(this.r, this.g, this.b)
    };
  }
  toCmykString() {
    const { c: t, m: l, y: n, k: i } = sa(this.r, this.g, this.b);
    return `cmyk(${t}, ${l}, ${n}, ${i})`;
  }
  /**
   * The 'real' name of the color -if there is one.
   */
  toName() {
    if (this.a === 0)
      return "transparent";
    if (this.a < 1)
      return !1;
    const t = "#" + ia(this.r, this.g, this.b, !1);
    for (const [l, n] of Object.entries(Li))
      if (t === n)
        return l;
    return !1;
  }
  toString(t) {
    const l = !!t;
    t = t ?? this.format;
    let n = !1;
    const i = this.a < 1 && this.a >= 0;
    return !l && i && (t.startsWith("hex") || t === "name") ? t === "name" && this.a === 0 ? this.toName() : this.toRgbString() : (t === "rgb" && (n = this.toRgbString()), t === "prgb" && (n = this.toPercentageRgbString()), (t === "hex" || t === "hex6") && (n = this.toHexString()), t === "hex3" && (n = this.toHexString(!0)), t === "hex4" && (n = this.toHex8String(!0)), t === "hex8" && (n = this.toHex8String()), t === "name" && (n = this.toName()), t === "hsl" && (n = this.toHslString()), t === "hsv" && (n = this.toHsvString()), t === "cmyk" && (n = this.toCmykString()), n || this.toHexString());
  }
  toNumber() {
    return (Math.round(this.r) << 16) + (Math.round(this.g) << 8) + Math.round(this.b);
  }
  clone() {
    return new Ve(this.toString());
  }
  /**
   * Lighten the color a given amount. Providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  lighten(t = 10) {
    const l = this.toHsl();
    return l.l += t / 100, l.l = yn(l.l), new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Brighten the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  brighten(t = 10) {
    const l = this.toRgb();
    return l.r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, l.r - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), l.g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, l.g - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), l.b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, l.b - Math.round(255 * -(t / 100)))), new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Darken the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  darken(t = 10) {
    const l = this.toHsl();
    return l.l -= t / 100, l.l = yn(l.l), new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure white, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return white.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  tint(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("white", t);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the color with pure black, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 0 will do nothing, providing 100 will always return black.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  shade(t = 10) {
    return this.mix("black", t);
  }
  /**
   * Desaturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * Providing 100 will is the same as calling greyscale
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  desaturate(t = 10) {
    const l = this.toHsl();
    return l.s -= t / 100, l.s = yn(l.s), new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Saturate the color a given amount, from 0 to 100.
   * @param amount - valid between 1-100
   */
  saturate(t = 10) {
    const l = this.toHsl();
    return l.s += t / 100, l.s = yn(l.s), new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Completely desaturates a color into greyscale.
   * Same as calling `desaturate(100)`
   */
  greyscale() {
    return this.desaturate(100);
  }
  /**
   * Spin takes a positive or negative amount within [-360, 360] indicating the change of hue.
   * Values outside of this range will be wrapped into this range.
   */
  spin(t) {
    const l = this.toHsl(), n = (l.h + t) % 360;
    return l.h = n < 0 ? 360 + n : n, new Ve(l);
  }
  /**
   * Mix the current color a given amount with another color, from 0 to 100.
   * 0 means no mixing (return current color).
   */
  mix(t, l = 50) {
    const n = this.toRgb(), i = new Ve(t).toRgb(), s = l / 100, o = {
      r: (i.r - n.r) * s + n.r,
      g: (i.g - n.g) * s + n.g,
      b: (i.b - n.b) * s + n.b,
      a: (i.a - n.a) * s + n.a
    };
    return new Ve(o);
  }
  analogous(t = 6, l = 30) {
    const n = this.toHsl(), i = 360 / l, s = [this];
    for (n.h = (n.h - (i * t >> 1) + 720) % 360; --t; )
      n.h = (n.h + i) % 360, s.push(new Ve(n));
    return s;
  }
  /**
   * taken from https://github.com/infusion/jQuery-xcolor/blob/master/jquery.xcolor.js
   */
  complement() {
    const t = this.toHsl();
    return t.h = (t.h + 180) % 360, new Ve(t);
  }
  monochromatic(t = 6) {
    const l = this.toHsv(), { h: n } = l, { s: i } = l;
    let { v: s } = l;
    const o = [], d = 1 / t;
    for (; t--; )
      o.push(new Ve({ h: n, s: i, v: s })), s = (s + d) % 1;
    return o;
  }
  splitcomplement() {
    const t = this.toHsl(), { h: l } = t;
    return [
      this,
      new Ve({ h: (l + 72) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l }),
      new Ve({ h: (l + 216) % 360, s: t.s, l: t.l })
    ];
  }
  /**
   * Compute how the color would appear on a background
   */
  onBackground(t) {
    const l = this.toRgb(), n = new Ve(t).toRgb(), i = l.a + n.a * (1 - l.a);
    return new Ve({
      r: (l.r * l.a + n.r * n.a * (1 - l.a)) / i,
      g: (l.g * l.a + n.g * n.a * (1 - l.a)) / i,
      b: (l.b * l.a + n.b * n.a * (1 - l.a)) / i,
      a: i
    });
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(3)`
   */
  triad() {
    return this.polyad(3);
  }
  /**
   * Alias for `polyad(4)`
   */
  tetrad() {
    return this.polyad(4);
  }
  /**
   * Get polyad colors, like (for 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, etc...)
   * monad, dyad, triad, tetrad, pentad, hexad, heptad, octad, etc...
   */
  polyad(t) {
    const l = this.toHsl(), { h: n } = l, i = [this], s = 360 / t;
    for (let o = 1; o < t; o++)
      i.push(new Ve({ h: (n + o * s) % 360, s: l.s, l: l.l }));
    return i;
  }
  /**
   * compare color vs current color
   */
  equals(t) {
    const l = new Ve(t);
    return this.format === "cmyk" || l.format === "cmyk" ? this.toCmykString() === l.toCmykString() : this.toRgbString() === l.toRgbString();
  }
}
function Ot(e, t = 20) {
  return e.mix("#141414", t).toString();
}

function S1(e, t) {
  const l = e.width * e.tileSize, n = e.height * e.tileSize, i = Math.min(t.w / l, t.h / n), s = l * i, o = n * i, d = t.x + (t.w - s) / 2, r = t.y + (t.h - o) / 2;
  return Object.freeze({
    scale: i,
    rect: Object.freeze({ x: d, y: r, w: s, h: o }),
    worldToDisplay(u, c) {
      return [d + u * i, r + c * i];
    },
    displayToWorld(u, c) {
      return u < d || c < r || u >= d + s || c >= r + o ? null : [(u - d) / i, (c - r) / i];
    },
    tileCenterToDisplay(u, c, y = 0, D = 0) {
      return this.worldToDisplay((u - y) * 16 + 8, (c - D) * 16 + 8);
    }
  });
}

}, Wo = 1 / 16, Bo = 4;
function Mt(e) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(e)) throw new RangeError("視口參數必須為有限數值");
  return e;
}
function Ih(e) {
  if (!e || Mt(e.width) <= 0 || Mt(e.height) <= 0) throw new RangeError("視口尺寸必須大於零");
}
function Sh(e) {
  if (Mt(e.x), Mt(e.y), Mt(e.zoom) < Wo || e.zoom > Bo) throw new RangeError("縮放超出工作區顯示範圍");
}
function Mh(e, t, l) {
  return Sh(e), [Mt(t) / e.zoom + e.x, Mt(l) / e.zoom + e.y];
}
function Ph(e, t, l) {
  Ih(t), Mt(l);
  const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(50, l));
  if (Mt(e[0]), Mt(e[1]), n === 0) return [0, 0];
  const i = (s, o) => {
    Mt(s);
    const d = Math.min(32, o / 4);
    return s < d ? -640 * n / 1e3 * Math.min(1, (d - s) / d) : s > o - d ? 640 * n / 1e3 * Math.min(1, (s - o + d) / d) : 0;
  };
  return [i(e[0], t.width), i(e[1], t.height)];
}

      if (g === "reset" && (X = "重設密碼"), !i(X + "帳戶 " + k.account + "？此操作會撤銷它的既有會話。")) return !1;
      const ae = O(), le = { expectedVersion: k.version };
      g === "disable" && (le.disabled = !k.disabled);
      try {
        await e("/api/admin/accounts/" + encodeURIComponent(k.id) + "/" + g, le);
      } catch (fe) {
        throw ae() && (c = !0, y = fe.message + " 原清單僅供核對，請重載後再操作；不自動重試。", W()), fe;
      }
      return ae() ? $() : !1;
    });
  }
  return Object.freeze({
    setIdentity(S) {
      S !== s && (s = S, o++, d++, r = [], u = !1, c = !1, y = "", W());
    },
    // load is used inside the client's already-serialized login/password flow.
    load: $,
    refresh: () => l($),
    create: A,
    mutate: V
  });
}
function Ow({ api: e, authority: t, work: l, isBusy: n, publish: i, gotoGame: s, openDeletion: o, storage: d = globalThis.sessionStorage, confirm: r = (u) => globalThis.confirm(u) }) {

  }
  function ke(Z) {
    return Z.listed ? "已上架" : Z.restricted ? "下架（管理限制，須管理員解除）" : "下架";
  }
  async function je(Z) {

    if (!ae(Z)) return;

    if (!Array.isArray(ge?.games)) throw new Error("遊戲清單回應無效。");
    W = ge.games.map((xe) => {
      if (typeof xe.gameId != "string" || !xe.gameId || Me.has(xe.gameId) || !k(xe.draftRevision) || typeof xe.metadata?.name != "string" || typeof xe.metadata.introduction != "string" || typeof xe.createdAt != "string" || typeof xe.modifiedAt != "string") throw new Error("遊戲清單回應無效。");

      const ze = ke(xe);
      return { gameId: xe.gameId, label: xe.metadata.name + " — " + (xe.metadata.introduction || "無簡介") + "／草稿 " + xe.draftRevision + "／" + ze };
    });
  }
  async function me(Z) {

      D = null;
      return;
    }

      if (A = Me.code === "COPY_OPERATION_REVOKED" ? "原操作已隨帳戶版本失效；本機追蹤不提供權限。" : "服務未找到此原鍵；這不是取消或回滾的證明。", !r(A + "只清除本機追蹤？這不取消或回滾可能在途的請求。") || !ae(Z) || y !== ge) return;

    }
  }

    !ae(ge) || y !== xe || (D = ee(ze), O = !1, await ve(ge));
  }
  async function ve(Z) {

    if (!ge) throw new Error("缺少原鍵，不能繼續。");

      O = !0, ce();
      const ze = await e("/api/games/create/" + encodeURIComponent(ge));
      if (!ae(Z) || y !== ge) return;
      D = ee(ze), O = !1;
    }
    if (D.draftRevision) {
      A = "完整副本已建立。", await je(Z), ce();
      return;
    }

      return;
    }

  }

      return await Z(...ge);

    }
  });
  function Be(Z = !1) {
    return !(x || $ || fe()) || !Z && r("新建輸入或原操作尚未完成。離開不會自動保存或取消服務操作；仍要離開？");
